([Fabric-Pro/fabric-oss](https://github.com/Fabric-Pro/fabric-oss/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/document-processing-pdf) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **document-processing-xlsx**: Process, parse, create, and manipulate Excel spreadsheets (.xlsx, .xls) using libraries like xlsx, exceljs, or SheetJS for data import/export and spreadsheet automation. Use when reading Excel files for data import, generating Excel reports, exporting data to spreadsheets, parsing bulk uploads, creating financial reports, manipulating workbooks and worksheets, or building data export features. ([Fabric-Pro/fabric-oss](https://github.com/Fabric-Pro/fabric-oss/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/document-processing-xlsx) · ★3 · 3 copies)
